CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
主席報告


BUSINESS REVIEW                                                                    業務回顧
On behalf of the board of directors (the “Board”), I am delighted to report the    本人謹代表董事會（「董事會」），欣然向各位股
best ever full-year performance on record of Kingboard Holdings Limited (the       東公佈，建滔集團有限公司及其附屬公司（「集
“Company”) and its subsidiaries (the “Group”) for the financial year ended         團」）於截至二零二一年十二月三十一日止財政
31 December 2021 (the “Period”). Reported net profit attributable to the           年度（「回顧期」）取得有紀錄以來最佳的全年業
shareholders of the Company broke through the milestone of HK$10,000               績，本公司持有人應佔賬面純利突破百億港元
million and reached HK$10,778 million.                                             大關，達一百零七億七千八百萬港元。

In 2021, the recovery of the global economy accelerated with a spike in            於二零二一年，環球經濟復甦步伐加快，大宗
the demand for major commodities and a bullish performance in the retail           商品的需求激增，零售市場需求強勁。憑藉集
market. On the back of the competitive edges of the Group’s operating              團垂直整合產業鏈之經營模式及多元化之業務
model featuring a vertically integrated value chain and a diversified business     組合的競爭優勢，以及在建滔同仁刻苦耐勞齊
portfolio, coupled with the industriousness, stamina and concerted effort from     心合力之下，集團業務大幅增長，各核心業務
top to bottom in Kingboard, the Group’s business grew by leaps and bounds          均取得了卓越的成績。
and each core unit obtained excellent results.

During the Period, the Group adopted a proactive sales and marketing               回顧期內，環球電子消費產品需求量激增，集
strategy to capture the upsurge in the demand for consumer electronics             團採取積極的營銷策略，擴展更深更廣的客戶
across the globe, whilst deepening and widening cooperation with clients, in       合作平臺，充分發揮覆銅面板部門垂直整合模
order to make full use of the vertically integrated model that the Laminates       式的優勢，以及覆銅面板與印刷線路板部門間
Division runs on, and the synergy and collaboration between the Laminates          強強聯手的協同效應。兩部門均實現了銷售數
and Printed Circuit Boards (“PCBs”) Divisions. Both divisions achieved             量和產品價格的雙線豐收。化工部門受益於市
fruitful results with regard to sales volume and product pricing. Benefiting       場需求旺盛，集團以醋酸，燒鹼及雙酚A為代表
from substantial market demand, major chemical products in the Chemicals           的主要化工產品價格錄得可觀升幅，帶動部門
Division, represented by acetic acid, caustic soda and Bisphenol A, recorded       收穫豐厚成果。地產部門在住宅預售及租金收
considerable price increases, driving the division’s strong results. The           入方面均有穩健表現。集團多元化業務組合一
Property Division saw stable performance in both residential pre-sale and          同發力，各部門都取得非常驕人的成績。
rental income. With each division working together to produce impressive
results, the Group’s diversified business portfolio is working to its advantage.

Principal Risks and Uncertainties                                                   主要風險及不明確因素
The Group is exposed to various risks and uncertainties which are specific to       本集團面臨多項本集團及╱或本集團經營所在
the Group and/or the industries in which the Group operates. These risks may        行業特有的風險及不明確因素。此等風險可能
materially affect the Group’s business operations, financial condition, results     會重大影響本集團的業務營運、財務狀況、營
of operations and business prospects. The Group has identified the key risks        運業績及業務前景。本集團已識別出以下的主
and uncertainties as follows:                                                       要風險及不明確因素：

Product defects                                                                     產品缺陷
The products of the Group may contain defects that can only be detected             本集團產品可能帶有缺陷，要待產品被裝設到
when the electronics systems into which they are incorporated are in use.           電子系統裡投用時方能被發現。倘若本集團產
The Group could be exposed to significant liability claims in the event that its    品被發現有缺陷時，我們可能會受到重大的責
products are found to be defective. While the Group has implemented sound           任索償。雖然本集團已設有穩妥的體制在不同
systems to monitor its products at various stages of its production processes,      生產階段中監察產品，概不保證本集團的產品
no assurance can be given that the Group’s products are free of defects.            毫無缺陷。如有重大責任索償，可對本集團的
Any significant liability claims could have an adverse impact on the results of     營運業績及聲望帶來不利影響。
operations and reputation of the Group.

Customer contracts                                                                  客戶合約
The Group typically enters into one-off purchase orders with its customers. As      本集團一般與客戶訂立一次性的購買訂單。因
such, the amount of purchase orders may vary significantly from time to time,       此，購買訂單的金額不時會有大額差異，難以
and it is difficult to forecast the amount of orders to be received by the Group    預測本集團未來會收到的訂單金額。概不保證
in the future. No assurance can be given that the Group’s customers will            本集團客戶在未來會繼續下達金額與先前期間
continue to place purchase orders with it in the future in similar amounts to       相若的購買訂單，甚至可能完全不下達訂單。
prior periods, if at all. As a result, the results of operations of the Group may   因此，本集團的營運業績在日後或會有重大差
vary significantly in the future.                                                   異。

The Group’s Investment Strategy for These Investments                            本集團就該等投資的投資策略
Our investment portfolio is comprised mainly of listed securities of a variety   我們的投資組合主要包括多間藍籌上市公司的
of blue-chip listed companies and debt securities issued by creditable           上市證券及有信貸評級良好的香港上市公司的
listed companies in Hong Kong. Our investment objective is to generate           債務證券。我們的投資目標為產生穩定的利息
stable interest and dividend income and create value for the Group and its       及股息收入，及為集團及股東創造價值。
shareholders.

listed company. It is in the interest of the stakeholders and shareholders for     上市公司之營運具透明度，採納各種自行規管
a listed company to operate in a transparent manner with the adoptions of          政策與程序以及監控機制，並清楚界定董事與
various self-regulatory policies, procedures and monitoring mechanisms and         管理層權責，乃符合權益持有人及股東之利益。
a clear definition of accountability of directors and management.

OPINION                                                                            意見
We have audited the consolidated financial statements of Kingboard Holdings        本核數師（以下簡稱「我們」）已審計載列於第
Limited (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries (collectively referred to as “the     68至248頁的建滔集團有限公司（以下簡稱「貴
Group”) set out on pages 68 to 248, which comprise the consolidated                公司」）及其附屬公司（統稱「貴集團」）的綜合財
statement of financial position as at 31 December 2021, and the consolidated       務報表，此財務報表包括於二零二一年十二月
statement of profit or loss and the consolidated statement of profit or loss and   三十一日的綜合財務狀況表與截至該日止年度
other comprehensive income, consolidated statement of changes in equity            的綜合損益表及綜合損益及其他全面收益表、
and consolidated statement of cash flows for the year then ended, and notes        綜合權益變動表和綜合現金流量表，以及綜合
to the consolidated financial statements, including a summary of significant       財務報表附註，包括主要會計政策概要。
accounting policies.

In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a true and fair view    我們認為，該等綜合財務報表已根據香港會計
of the consolidated financial position of the Group as at 31 December 2021,        師公會（「香港會計師公會」）頒布的《香港財務申
and of its consolidated financial performance and its consolidated cash flows      報準則》（「香港財務申報準則」）真實而中肯地反
for the year then ended in accordance with Hong Kong Financial Reporting           映了 貴集團於二零二一年十二月三十一日的
Standards (“HKFRSs”) issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public         綜合財務狀況及截至該日止年度的綜合財務表
Accountants (“HKICPA”) and have been properly prepared in compliance with          現及綜合現金流量，並已遵照香港《公司條例》
the disclosure requirements of the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.                  的披露要求妥為擬備。

of the Consolidated Financial Statements section of our report. We are             責任」部分中作進一步闡述。根據香港會計師公
independent of the Group in accordance with the HKICPA’s Code of Ethics            會頒布的《專業會計師道德守則》（ 以下簡稱「守

 我們將評審待發展物業的可變現淨值識別為關鍵審計事                                         我們評審待發展物業的可變現淨值的程序包括：
 項，此乃由於釐定其可變現淨值涉及重大金額及估計不確
 認性。                                                              •      透過獲取獨立合資格測量師有關已產生的成本及完成進度
                                                                         的直接確認，抽樣評估待發展物業確認的成本合理性；

         capitalisation of retained profits of certain subsidiaries established in the People’s          中華人民共和國（「中國」）成立的附屬公司之保留
         Republic of China (“PRC”) for capital re-investment in these subsidiaries and funds             溢利資本化作為於該等附屬公司之再投資資本，

1.    GENERAL INFORMATION                                                      1.   一般資料
      Kingboard Holdings Limited (the “Company”) is a public limited                建滔集團有限公司（「本公司」）在開曼群
      company incorporated in the Cayman Islands and its shares are listed          島註冊成立為上市有限公司，其股份在
      on the Main Board of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited                  香港聯合交易所有限公司（「聯交所」）之
      (the “Stock Exchange”). The addresses of the registered office and            主板上市。本公司註冊辦事處之地址及
      principal place of business of the Company are disclosed in the               主要營業地點於本年報「公司資料」一節
      “Corporate Information” section of the annual report.                         中披露。

      The Company is an investment holding company and the principal                本公司為投資控股公司，其附屬公司之
      activities of its subsidiaries are set out in Note 45.                        主要業務載於附註45。

      The principal activities of its subsidiaries are the manufacture and          其附屬公司的主要業務為製造及銷售覆
      sales of laminates, printed circuit boards (“PCBs”), chemicals,               銅面板、印刷線路板（「印刷線路板」）、
      magnetic products, property development and investments and                   化工產品、磁石產品、房地產發展及投
      investment business.                                                          資以及投資業務。

      The consolidated financial statements are presented in Hong Kong              綜合財務報表以港元（「港元」）呈列，港
      dollars (“HK$”), which is different from the Company’s functional             元有別於本公司的功能貨幣人民幣（「人
      currency of Renminbi (“RMB”), the currency of the primary economic            民幣」），即本公司經營所在的主要經濟
      environment in which the Company operates. However, because the               環境的貨幣。然而，由於本公司股份於
      Company’s shares are listed on the Stock Exchange, where most of              聯交所上市，其大部分投資者均位於香
      its investors are located in Hong Kong, therefore the directors of the        港，因此本公司董事（「董事」）認為以港
      Company (“Directors”) considered it is more convenient for the users          元呈列更方便綜合財務報表的使用者。
      of the consolidated financial statements.

     HONG KONG FINANCIAL REPORTING                                                 申報準則（「香港財務申
     STANDARDS (“HKFRSs”) (continued)                                              報準則」）
                                                                                       （續）

     Amendments to HKFRSs that are mandatorily effective                           於本年度已強制生效之經修訂之
     for the current year (continued)                                              香港財務申報準則（續）
     Impacts on application of Amendments to HKFRS 9, HKAS 39,                     香港財務申報準則第9號、香港會計準
     HKFRS 7, HKFRS 4 and HKFRS 16 “Interest Rate Benchmark                        則第39號、香港財務申報準則第7號、
     Reform – Phase 2” (continued)                                                 香港財務申報準則第4號及香港財務申
                                                                                   報 準 則 第 16 號（ 修 訂 本 ）
                                                                                                       「利率基準改
                                                                                   革－第二階段」之影響（續）
     The amendments have had no impact on the consolidated financial               由於概無相關合約已於本年度過渡到相
     statements as none of the relevant contracts has been transitioned to         關替代利率，該等修訂本對綜合財務報
     the relevant replacement rates during the year. The Group will apply          表並無影響。本集團將對按攤銷成本列
     the practical expedient in relation to the changes in contractual cash        賬之銀行貸款的合約現金流量因利率基
     flows resulting from the interest rate benchmark reform for bank loans        準改革而產生之變動採用可行權宜方
     measured at amortised cost. Additional disclosures as required by             法。香港財務申報準則第7號所要求的
     HKFRS 7 are set out in Note 36.                                               披露於附註36闡述。

     Impacts on application of the agenda decision of the                          應用委員會─出售存貨所需之成本之議
     Committee – Cost necessary to sell inventories (HKAS 2                        程決定（香港會計準則第2號「存貨」
                                                                                                   （「香
     “Inventories” (“HKAS 2”))                                                     港會計準則第2號」
                                                                                           ））之影響
     In June 2021, the Committee, through its agenda decision, clarified           於二零二一年六月，委員會通過其議程
     the costs an entity should include as “estimated costs necessary              決定釐清實體在釐定存貨之可變現淨值
     to make the sale” when determining the net realisable value of                時之成本應列為「進行銷售所需之估計
     inventories. In particular, whether such costs should be limited to           成本」。尤其是，有關成本是否應僅限
     those that are incremental to the sale. The Committee concluded that          於銷售增量成本。委員會認為，進行
     the estimated costs necessary to make the sale should not be limited          銷售所需之估計成本不應僅限於增量成
     to those that are incremental but should also include costs that an           本，亦應包括實體出售其存貨須承擔之
     entity must incur to sell its inventories including those that are not        成本，包括非特定銷售增量成本。
     incremental to a particular sale.

     The Group’s accounting policy prior to the Committee’s agenda                 本集團於委員會議程決定前之會計政策
     decision was to determine the net realisable value of inventories             為僅計及增量成本釐定可變現淨值。於
     taking into consideration incremental costs only. Upon application of         應用委員會議程決定後，本集團改變其
     the Committee’s agenda decision, the Group changed its accounting             會計政策，在釐定存貨之可變現淨值時
     policy to determine the net realisable value of inventories taking            計及增量成本及出售存貨所需的其他成
     into consideration both incremental costs and other cost necessary            本。新會計政策已獲追溯應用。
     to sell inventories. The new accounting policy has been applied
     retrospectively.

     The application of the Committee’s agenda decision has had no                 應用委員會議程決定對本集團財務狀況
     material impact on the Group’s financial positions and performance.           及表現並無重大影響。

           whether that price is directly observable or estimated using                   不論該價格為可直接觀察取得或
           another valuation technique. In estimating the fair value                      可使用其他估值方法估計。於估
           of an asset or a liability, the Group takes into account the                   計資產或負債之公平值時，本集

           would take those characteristics into account when pricing                     日期對資產或負債定價時所考慮
           the asset or liability at the measurement date. Fair value                     之資產或負債之特點。於該等綜
           for measurement and/or disclosure purposes in these                            合財務報表中作計量及╱或披露
           consolidated financial statements is determined on such a                      用途之公平值乃按此基準釐定，

           are within the scope of HKFRS 2 “Share-based Payment”,                         務申報準則第2號「以股份形式
           leasing transactions that are accounted for in accordance with                 付款」範圍內之以股份形式付款
           HKFRS 16 “Leases” (“HKFRS 16”), and measurements that                          交易、按照香港財務申報準則第
           have some similarities to fair value but are not fair value, such              16號「租賃」

           Basis of consolidation                                                         綜合賬目基準
           The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial                綜合財務報表包括本公司及由本
           statements of the Company and entities controlled by the                       公司及其附屬公司控制之實體之
           Company and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved when the                     財務報表。當本公司符合以下情
           Company:                                                                       況，即取得控制權：

           •       has power over the investee;                                           •   有權控制被投資方；

           •       is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its                •   因其參與被投資方業務
                   involvement with the investee; and                                         而對可變回報承擔風險
                                                                                              或享有權利；及

           •       has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.                •   有能力以其權力影響其
                                                                                              回報。

           The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee                    倘有事實及情況顯示上列三項控
           if facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to                  制權條件之其中一項或多項有
           one or more of the three elements of control listed above.                     變，本集團會重新評估其是否控
                                                                                          制被投資方。

           Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Group obtains                    當本集團取得附屬公司之控制
           control over the subsidiary and ceases when the Group loses                    權，便將該附屬公司綜合入賬；
           control of the subsidiary. Specifically, income and expenses                   當本集團失去附屬公司之控制
           of a subsidiary acquired or disposed of during the year are                    權，便停止將該附屬公司綜合入
           included in the consolidated statement of profit or loss and                   賬。具體而言，年內所收購或出
           from the date the Group gains control until the date when the                  售附屬公司之收入及開支於本集
           Group ceases to control the subsidiary.                                        團獲得控制權日期計入綜合損益
                                                                                          表，直至本集團不再控制該附屬
                                                                                          公司為止。

           Profit or loss and each item of other comprehensive income                     損益及其他全面收益的各個項目
           are attributed to the owners of the Company and to the                         乃歸屬於本公司持有人及非控股

           subsidiaries is attributed to the owners of the Company and                    乃歸屬於本公司持有人及非控股

           controlling interests having a deficit balance.                                益產生虧絀結餘。

           line with the Group’s accounting policies.                                     政策與本集團會計政策貫徹一

           (if any) are derecognised. A gain or loss is recognised in                         接於權益內確認，並由本公司持
           profit or loss and is calculated as the difference between (i)                     有人分佔。收益或虧損會於損益

           The Group recognises revenue when (or as) a performance                        本集團於履行履約義務時（或就

           transferred to the customer.                                                   轉移予客戶時確認收益。

           A performance obligation represents a good or service (or                      履約責任指一項明確貨品或服務
           a bundle of goods or services) that is distinct or a series of                 （或一批貨品或服務）或一系列大
           distinct goods or services that are substantially the same.                    致相同之明確貨品或服務。

           Control is transferred over time and revenue is recognised                     倘符合下列其中一項標準，貨品
           over time by reference to the progress towards complete                        或服務的控制權在一段時間內轉
           satisfaction of the relevant performance obligation if one of the              移，而我們會按一段時間內已完
           following criteria is met:                                                     成相關履約責任的進度確認收
                                                                                          益：

           •       the customer simultaneously receives and consumes                      •   客戶同時收取及耗用由
                   the benefits provided by the Group’s performance as                        本集團履約所帶來的利
                   the Group performs;                                                        益；

           •       the Group’s performance creates or enhances                            •   本集團履約導致創建或
                   an asset that the customer controls as the Group                           提升於本集團履約時由
                   performs; or                                                               客戶控制的資產；或

           •       the Group’s performance does not create an asset                       •   本集團履約並無產生對
                   with an alternative use to the Group and the Group                         本集團有替代用途的資
                   has an enforceable right to payment for performance                        產，且本集團可享有強
                   completed to date.                                                         制執行權，以收回至今
                                                                                              已履約部分的款項。

           Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time when the                   否則，收益於客戶獲得明確商品
           customer obtains control of the distinct good or service.                      或服務的控制權時確認。

           Leases                                                                       租賃
           Definition of a lease                                                        租賃的定義
           A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract conveys the              倘合約為換取代價而給予在一段
           right to control the use of an identified asset for a period of              時間內控制使用可識別資產的權
           time in exchange for consideration.                                          利，則該合約屬租賃或包含租

            The Group performs impairment assessment under expected                       本集團根據香港財務申報準則第
            credit loss (“ECL”) model on financial assets (including trade                9號的減值規定，按預期信貸虧
            receivables, other receivables, bills receivables, entrusted                  損（「預期信貸虧損」）模型對金融
            loans, loan receivable, debt instruments at FVTOCI and bank                   資產（包括貿易應收賬款、其他
            balances) and financial guarantee contracts which are subject                 應收賬款、應收票據、委託貸
            to impairment assessment under HKFRS 9. The amount of                         款、應收貸款、按公平值計入其
            ECL is updated at each reporting date to reflect changes in                   他全面收益的債務工具及銀行結
            credit risk since initial recognition.                                        餘）和財務擔保進行減值評估。
                                                                                          預期信貸虧損金額於各報告日期
                                                                                          更新，以反映自首次確認以來信
                                                                                          貸風險的變化。

            Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that will result from                         全期預期信貸虧損指將因相關工
            all possible default events over the expected life of the                     具預計存續期內所有可能違約事
            relevant instrument. In contrast, 12-month ECL (“12m ECL”)                    件而導致的預期信貸虧損。12
            represents the portion of lifetime ECL that is expected to                    個月預期信貸虧損（「12個月預
            result from default events that are possible within 12 months                 期信貸虧損」）指預計因報告日期
            after the reporting date. Assessments are done based on the                   後12個月內可能出現的違約事
            Group’s historical credit loss experience, adjusted for factors               件導致的全期預期信貸虧損的一
            that are specific to the debtors, general economic conditions                 部分。評估基於本集團的過往信
            and an assessment of both the current conditions at the                       用損失經驗作出，並就債務人特
            reporting date as well as the forecast of future conditions.                  定因素、整體經濟狀況及報告日
                                                                                          期現行狀況的評估及未來狀況預
                                                                                          測而作出調整。

            The Group always recognises lifetime ECL for trade                            本集團始終就貿易應收賬款確認
            receivables. The ECL on these assets are assessed individually                全期預期信貸虧損。該等資產的
            and/or collectively based on appropriate groupings.                           預期信貸虧損進行單項評估及╱
                                                                                          或使用適當分組的組合評估。

            For all other instruments, the Group measures the loss                        就所有其他工具而言，本集團計
            allowance equal to 12m ECL, unless there has been a                           量等於12個月預期信貸虧損的
            significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition,                減值撥備，除非自初始確認起信
            in which case the Group recognises lifetime ECL. The                          貸風險顯著增加，則本集團確認
            assessment of whether lifetime ECL should be recognised                       全期預期信貸虧損。對是否應確

                   as at the date of initial recognition. In making this                     認日期發生違約的風險

                   supportable, including historical experience and                          並有支持的定量及定性

                                                                                             成本或努力即可獲得的
                                                                                             前瞻性信息。

                   of default and loss given default is based on historical                      對違約概率及違約損失

                   ECL reflects an unbiased and probability-weighted                             和前瞻性信息。預期信

                                                                                                 重的無偏概率加權金額。

      obtained, which is the point of time when the goods are delivered               業額乃於獲得客戶接受時確認，即按協
      based on the agreed shipping terms and the location specified by the            定運送條款及客戶指定位置交付貨品，
      customers, and when the customer has the ability to direct the use of           以及當客戶有能力主導該等產品的使用
      these products and obtain substantially all of the remaining benefits of        及獲得該等產品絕大部分餘下利益的時
      these products.                                                                 間點。

      The revenue of the Group arising from sales of properties in the                本集團於日常業務過程中銷售物業產生
      ordinary course of business is recognised at a point in time when the           的收益於客戶獲得有關物業的控制權之
      customer obtains control of the respective properties.                          時間點予以確認。

      Revenue from hotel accommodation are recognised over time, as                   酒店住宿營業額隨時間確認，因為客戶
      the customer simultaneously receives and consumes the benefits                  隨本集團履約同時收取及耗用由本集團
      provided by the Group’s performance as the Group performs.                      履約所帶來的利益。

6.    SEGMENT INFORMATION                                                           6.   分部資料
      HKFRS 8 “Operating Segments” (“HKFRS 8”) requires operating                         香 港 財 務 申 報 準 則 第 8 號「 經 營 分 部 」
      segments to be identified on the basis of internal reports about                   （「香港財務申報準則第8號」）要求以集
      components of the Group that are regularly reviewed by the executive                團執行董事為主要營運決策者（「主要營
      directors of the Company, who are the Chief Operating Decision                      運決策者」）在對分部作資源分配及評估
      Makers (“CODM”), in order to allocate resources to segments and                     其表現上所定期審閱的有關集團不同部
      to assess their performance. Specifically, the Group’s reportable                   門之內部報告作為確定經營分部之基
      segments under HKFRS 8 are organised into six main operating                        準。具體而言，在香港財務申報準則第
      divisions – (i) laminates, (ii) PCBs, (iii) chemicals, (iv) properties, (v)         8號下，本集團之申報分部分為六個主
      investments (mainly investment income from debt instruments at                      要經營分部－(i)覆銅面板、(ii)印刷線路
      FVTOCI and equity instruments at FVTPL) and (vi) others (mainly                     板、(iii)化工產品、(iv)物業、(v)投資（主
      including service income, manufacture and sale of magnetic products                 要包括按公平值計入其他全面收益的債
      and hotel business).                                                                務工具及按公平值計入損益的權益工具
                                                                                          的投資收入）及(vi)其他（主要包括服務收
                                                                                          入、製造及銷售磁電產品及酒店業務）             。

      The accounting policies the Group used for segment reporting under                 本集團根據香港財務申報準則第8號用
      HKFRS 8 are the same as those used in its HKFRS consolidated                       作分部呈報之會計政策，與其用於香港
      financial statements. Segment profit or loss represents the profit                 財務申報準則之綜合財務報表一致。
      (loss) earned by each segment with certain items not included (share               分部之溢利或虧損代表各分部賺取的利
      of result of an associate, share of results of joint ventures, gain on             潤，但未有包括若干項目（應佔聯營公
      disposal of subsidiaries, finance costs and unallocated corporate                  司業績、應佔合營公司業績、出售附屬
      income and expenses). This is the measure reported to the CODM for                 公司之收益、融資成本及未分配之公司
      the purpose of resource allocation and performance assessment.                     收入及支出）。這是報告給主要營運決
                                                                                         策者用於資源分配和績效評估的措施。

14. INVESTMENT PROPERTIES (continued)                                            14. 投資物業（續）
      The fair value of the Group’s investment properties at 31 December           本集團投資物業於二零二一年十二月
      2021 had been arrived at on the basis of a valuation carried out by          三十一日之公平值乃按與本集團並無關
      Ravia Global Appraisal Advisory Limited, an independent qualified            連之獨立合資格估值師瑞豐環球評估諮
      valuer not connected to the Group. Ravia Global Appraisal Advisory           詢有限公司進行估值之基準達致。瑞豐
      Limited is a member of the Hong Kong Institute of Surveyors. The             環球評估諮詢有限公司為香港測量師學
      valuation was determined based on direct comparison method by                會會員。估值乃按直接比較法參考類似
      reference to the market observable transactions of similar properties        物業之可觀察市場交易並作出調整以反
      and adjusted to reflect conditions and locations of subject properties.      映目標物業之狀況及位置後釐定。於過
      There has been no change from the valuation technique used in prior          往年度所用之估值技術並無變動。
      years.

      In determining the fair value of the relevant properties, the Directors      為釐定相關物業的公平值，董事會已成
      have set up a valuation committee, which is headed up by the                 立估值委員會，由負責本集團財務事務
      persons in charge of financial matters of the Group, to determine            的人士帶領，以就公平值計量釐定適當
      the appropriate valuation techniques and inputs for fair value               的估值技術及輸入數據。
      measurements.

      The Group engages an independent valuer to perform its valuations.           本集團委聘獨立估值師估值。估值委員
      The valuation committee works closely with the independent valuer            會與獨立估值師緊密合作，共同制定模
      to establish the appropriate valuation techniques and inputs to the          型之適當估值技術及輸入數據。財務事
      model. The persons in charge of financial matters report the valuation       宜負責人每季向董事匯報估值委員會之
      committee’s findings to the Directors every quarter to explain the           發現，闡明資產公平值波動之因由。
      cause of fluctuations in the fair value of the assets.

      The Group’s investment properties were classified as Level 3 of the          本集團投資物業於二零二一年及二零二
      fair value hierarchy as at 31 December 2021 and 2020.                        零年十二月三十一日分類為第三級公平
                                                                                   值架構級別。

      The following table gives information about how the fair values               下表載列該等投資物業公平值的釐定方

      hierarchy into which the fair value measurements are categorised              度將公平值計量分類之公平值架構級別

35. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                     35. 資本風險管理
      The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the Group will     資本風險管理本集團管理其資本以確保
      be able to continue as a going concern while maximising the return to       通過優化債務與股本結存為權益持有人
      stakeholders through the optimisation of the debt and equity balance.       爭取最高回報，使本集團旗下實體能夠
      The Group’s overall strategy remains unchanged from prior year.             持續經營。本集團之整體策略與過往年
                                                                                  度相同。

      The capital structure of the Group consists of bank borrowings              誠如綜合權益變動表所披露，本集團之
      and lease liabilities, net of cash and cash equivalents and equity          資本結構包括銀行借貸及租賃負債（扣
      attributable to owners of the Company, comprising issued share              除現金及現金等價物）及本公司持有人
      capital, other reserves and retained profits as disclosed in the            應佔權益（包括已發行股本、其他儲備
      consolidated statement of changes in equity.                                及保留溢利）。

      The Directors review the capital structure on a semi-annual basis.          董事每半年審閱資本結構。作為審閱一
      As part of this review, the Directors consider the cost of capital and      部分，董事將考慮資本成本，及與各類
      the risks associated with each class of capital. In the opinion of the      別資本相關之風險。董事認為，本集團
      Directors, the Group will balance its overall capital structure through     將通過支付股息、發行新股、購回股
      the payment of dividends, new share issues and share buy-backs as           份、發行新債或者贖回現有債項，以平
      well as the issue of new debt or the redemption of existing debt.           衡整體資本結構。
